{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /logs/agent/sessions/skills/dc-power-flow\n\n# DC Power Flow\n\nDC power flow is a linearized approximation of AC power flow, suitable for economic dispatch and contingency analysis.\n\n## DC Approximations\n\n1. **Lossless lines** - Ignore resistance (R ≈ 0)\n2. **Flat voltage** - All bus voltages = 1.0 pu\n3. **Small angles** - sin(θ) ≈ θ, cos(θ) ≈ 1\n\nResult: Power flow depends only on bus angles (θ) and line reactances (X).\n\n## Bus Number Mapping\n\nPower system bus numbers may not be contiguous (e.g., case300 has non-sequential bus IDs). Always create a mapping from bus numbers to 0-indexed array positions:\n\n```python\n# Create mapping: bus_number -> 0-indexed position\nbus_num_to_idx = {int(buses[i, 0]): i for i in range(n_bus)}\n\n# Use mapping for branch endpoints\nf = bus_num_to_idx[int(br[0])]  # NOT br[0] - 1\nt = bus_num_to_idx[int(br[1])]\n```\n\n## Susceptance Matrix (B)\n\nBuild from branch reactances using bus number mapping:\n\n```python\n# Run: scripts/build_b_matrix.py\n# Or inline:\nbus_num_to_idx = {int(buses[i, 0]): i for i in range(n_bus)}\nB = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus))\n\nfor br in branches:\n    f = bus_num_to_idx[int(br[0])]  # Map bus number to index\n    t = bus_num_to_idx[int(br[1])]\n    x = br[3]  # Reactance\n    if x != 0:\n        b = 1.0 / x\n        B[f, f] += b\n        B[t, t] += b\n        B[f, t] -= b\n        B[t, f] -= b\n```\n\n## Power Balance Equation\n\nAt each bus: `Pg - Pd = B[i, :] @ θ`\n\nWhere:\n- Pg = generation at bus (pu)\n- Pd = load at bus (pu)\n- θ = vector of bus angles (radians)\n\n## Slack Bus\n\nOne bus must have θ = 0 as reference. Find slack bus (type=3):\n\n```python\nslack_idx = None\nfor i in range(n_bus):\n    if buses[i, 1] == 3:\n        slack_idx = i\n        break\nconstraints.append(theta[slack_idx] == 0)\n```\n\n## Line Flow Calculation\n\nFlow on branch from bus f to bus t (use bus number mapping):\n\n```python\nf = bus_num_to_idx[int(br[0])]\nt = bus_num_to_idx[int(br[1])]\nb = 1.0 / br[3]  # Susceptance = 1/X\nflow_pu = b * (theta[f] - theta[t])\nflow_MW = flow_pu * baseMVA\n```\n\n## Line Loading Percentage\n\n```python\nloading_pct = abs(flow_MW) / rating_MW * 100\n```\n\nWhere `rating_MW = branch[5]` (RATE_A column).\n\n## Branch Susceptances for Constraints\n\nStore susceptances when building constraints:\n\n```python\nbranch_susceptances = []\nfor br in branches:\n    x = br[3]\n    b = 1.0 / x if x != 0 else 0\n    branch_susceptances.append(b)\n```\n\n## Line Flow Limits (for OPF)\n\nEnforce thermal limits as linear constraints:\n\n```python\n# |flow| <= rating  →  -rating <= flow <= rating\nflow = b * (theta[f] - theta[t]) * baseMVA\nconstraints.append(flow <= rate)\nconstraints.append(flow >= -rate)\n```\n"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"e611fd13-74ca-4acc-9e36-98ff693673eb","uuid":"694602f8-fb13-40e4-9a5c-a9a4de7c883b","isSynthetic":true}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-5-20251101","id":"msg_vrtx_014G8krSgaiHL1mJh76Cm4Jw","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_01M8tH9n84MZ39pgxascZdVa","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/dc_opf_analysis.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nDC-OPF with Reserve Co-optimization for Market Analysis\nCompares base case with counterfactual scenario (20% increased line capacity on bus 64-1501)\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nimport cvxpy as cp\n\ndef load_network(filepath):\n    \"\"\"Load MATPOWER-format network from JSON\"\"\"\n    with open(filepath, 'r') as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n    return data\n\ndef run_dc_opf_with_reserves(data, line_capacity_override=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run DC-OPF with reserve co-optimization.\n\n    Args:\n        data: Network data dictionary\n        line_capacity_override: Dict of {(from_bus, to_bus): new_capacity} to override\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with solution results including LMPs and reserve MCP\n    \"\"\"\n    baseMVA = data['baseMVA']\n    buses = np.array(data['bus'])\n    gens = np.array(data['gen'])\n    branches = np.array(data['branch'])\n    gencosts = np.array(data['gencost'])\n    reserve_capacities = np.array(data['reserve_capacity'])\n    reserve_requirement = data['reserve_requirement']\n\n    n_bus = len(buses)\n    n_gen = len(gens)\n    n_branch = len(branches)\n\n    # Create bus number to index mapping\n    bus_num_to_idx = {int(buses[i, 0]): i for i in range(n_bus)}\n\n    # Find slack bus (type = 3)\n    slack_idx = None\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        if buses[i, 1] == 3:\n            slack_idx = i\n            break\n    if slack_idx is None:\n        slack_idx = 0  # Default to first bus\n\n    # Get generator bus indices\n    gen_bus_indices = [bus_num_to_idx[int(gens[g, 0])] for g in range(n_gen)]\n\n    # Get generator parameters (only in-service generators)\n    gen_status = gens[:, 7]\n    gen_pmax = gens[:, 8]  # MW\n    gen_pmin = gens[:, 9]  # MW\n\n    # Get load at each bus (column 2 is PD in MW)\n    bus_load = buses[:, 2].copy()  # MW\n\n    # Build susceptance matrix B\n    B = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus))\n    branch_susceptances = []\n    branch_from_idx = []\n    branch_to_idx = []\n    branch_limits = []\n    branch_status = []\n\n    for i, br in enumerate(branches):\n        f_bus = int(br[0])\n        t_bus = int(br[1])\n        x = br[3]  # Reactance\n        rate_a = br[5]  # MW limit\n        status = br[10]\n\n        f = bus_num_to_idx[f_bus]\n        t = bus_num_to_idx[t_bus]\n\n        branch_from_idx.append(f)\n        branch_to_idx.append(t)\n        branch_status.append(status)\n\n        # Check for line capacity override\n        if line_capacity_override is not None:\n            if (f_bus, t_bus) in line_capacity_override:\n                rate_a = line_capacity_override[(f_bus, t_bus)]\n            elif (t_bus, f_bus) in line_capacity_override:\n                rate_a = line_capacity_override[(t_bus, f_bus)]\n\n        branch_limits.append(rate_a)\n\n        if x != 0 and status == 1:\n            b = 1.0 / x\n            B[f, f] += b\n            B[t, t] += b\n            B[f, t] -= b\n            B[t, f] -= b\n            branch_susceptances.append(b)\n        else:\n            branch_susceptances.append(0.0)\n\n    branch_susceptances = np.array(branch_susceptances)\n    branch_from_idx = np.array(branch_from_idx)\n    branch_to_idx = np.array(branch_to_idx)\n    branch_limits = np.array(branch_limits)\n    branch_status = np.array(branch_status)\n\n    # Decision variables\n    Pg = cp.Variable(n_gen, name=\"Pg\")  # Generator real power output (MW)\n    Rg = cp.Variable(n_gen, name=\"Rg\")  # Generator reserve provision (MW)\n    theta = cp.Variable(n_bus, name=\"theta\")  # Bus voltage angles (radians)\n\n    # Cost function: polynomial cost (MODEL=2, NCOST=3 means c2*Pg^2 + c1*Pg + c0)\n    # gencost format: [MODEL, STARTUP, SHUTDOWN, NCOST, c2, c1, c0]\n    total_cost = 0\n    for g in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[g] == 1:\n            c2 = gencosts[g, 4]\n            c1 = gencosts[g, 5]\n            c0 = gencosts[g, 6]\n            # Quadratic cost: c2*Pg^2 + c1*Pg + c0\n            total_cost += c2 * cp.square(Pg[g]) + c1 * Pg[g] + c0\n\n    # Constraints\n    constraints = []\n\n    # 1. Slack bus angle reference\n    constraints.append(theta[slack_idx] == 0)\n\n    # 2. Generator limits (Pmin <= Pg <= Pmax - Rg)\n    for g in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[g] == 1:\n            constraints.append(Pg[g] >= gen_pmin[g])\n            constraints.append(Pg[g] + Rg[g] <= gen_pmax[g])\n            constraints.append(Rg[g] >= 0)\n            constraints.append(Rg[g] <= reserve_capacities[g])\n        else:\n            constraints.append(Pg[g] == 0)\n            constraints.append(Rg[g] == 0)\n\n    # 3. Power balance at each bus: sum(Pg at bus) - Pd = sum(B[i,j] * theta[j])\n    # Build injection vector\n    Pnet = np.zeros(n_bus)  # Will be modified by Pg\n\n    # For DC power flow: P_injection = B @ theta\n    # At each bus: Pg - Pd = sum over j of B[i,j]*theta[j]\n\n    # Create generation at bus matrix (n_bus x n_gen)\n    gen_at_bus = np.zeros((n_bus, n_gen))\n    for g in range(n_gen):\n        gen_at_bus[gen_bus_indices[g], g] = 1.0\n\n    # Power balance constraint: gen_at_bus @ Pg - Pd = B @ theta\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        gen_injection = gen_at_bus[i, :] @ Pg\n        constraints.append(gen_injection - bus_load[i] == B[i, :] @ theta * baseMVA)\n\n    # 4. Line flow limits: |b * (theta_f - theta_t) * baseMVA| <= rate\n    for i in range(n_branch):\n        if branch_status[i] == 1 and branch_limits[i] > 0:\n            f = branch_from_idx[i]\n            t = branch_to_idx[i]\n            b = branch_susceptances[i]\n            flow = b * (theta[f] - theta[t]) * baseMVA\n            constraints.append(flow <= branch_limits[i])\n            constraints.append(flow >= -branch_limits[i])\n\n    # 5. Reserve requirement: sum(Rg) >= reserve_requirement\n    reserve_constraint = cp.sum(Rg) >= reserve_requirement\n    constraints.append(reserve_constraint)\n\n    # Solve the optimization problem\n    problem = cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(total_cost), constraints)\n    problem.solve(solver=cp.ECOS, verbose=False)\n\n    if problem.status not in ['optimal', 'optimal_inaccurate']:\n        raise RuntimeError(f\"Optimization failed with status: {problem.status}\")\n\n    # Extract results\n    Pg_opt = Pg.value\n    Rg_opt = Rg.value\n    theta_opt = theta.value\n\n    # Calculate LMPs from power balance constraint duals\n    # LMP at bus i = dual of power balance constraint at bus i\n    lmps = []\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        # The dual value of the power balance equality constraint\n        dual = constraints[1 + 4*n_gen + i].dual_value\n        if dual is not None:\n            lmp = -dual  # Negative because of constraint formulation\n        else:\n            lmp = 0.0\n        lmps.append(lmp)\n    lmps = np.array(lmps)\n\n    # Calculate reserve MCP from reserve requirement constraint dual\n    reserve_constraint_idx = 1 + 4*n_gen + n_bus + 2*sum(1 for i in range(n_branch) if branch_status[i] == 1 and branch_limits[i] > 0)\n    reserve_mcp = constraints[reserve_constraint_idx].dual_value\n    if reserve_mcp is None:\n        reserve_mcp = 0.0\n    else:\n        reserve_mcp = float(reserve_mcp)\n\n    # Calculate line flows and identify binding lines\n    line_flows = []\n    binding_lines = []\n    for i in range(n_branch):\n        if branch_status[i] == 1:\n            f = branch_from_idx[i]\n            t = branch_to_idx[i]\n            b = branch_susceptances[i]\n            flow = b * (theta_opt[f] - theta_opt[t]) * baseMVA\n            line_flows.append(flow)\n\n            loading = abs(flow) / branch_limits[i] * 100 if branch_limits[i] > 0 else 0\n            if loading >= 99.0:\n                f_bus = int(branches[i, 0])\n                t_bus = int(branches[i, 1])\n                binding_lines.append({\n                    'from': f_bus,\n                    'to': t_bus,\n                    'flow_MW': round(abs(flow), 2),\n                    'limit_MW': round(branch_limits[i], 2),\n                    'loading_pct': round(loading, 2)\n                })\n        else:\n            line_flows.append(0.0)\n\n    # Build LMP by bus result\n    lmp_by_bus = []\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        bus_num = int(buses[i, 0])\n        lmp_by_bus.append({\n            'bus': bus_num,\n            'lmp_dollars_per_MWh': round(lmps[i], 6)\n        })\n\n    return {\n        'total_cost': problem.value,\n        'lmp_by_bus': lmp_by_bus,\n        'lmps': lmps,\n        'reserve_mcp': reserve_mcp,\n        'binding_lines': binding_lines,\n        'Pg': Pg_opt,\n        'Rg': Rg_opt,\n        'theta': theta_opt,\n        'buses': buses,\n        'status': problem.status\n    }\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading network data...\")\n    data = load_network('/root/network.json')\n\n    print(f\"Network: {data['name']}\")\n    print(f\"Buses: {len(data['bus'])}, Generators: {len(data['gen'])}, Branches: {len(data['branch'])}\")\n    print(f\"Reserve requirement: {data['reserve_requirement']} MW\")\n\n    # Find the target line (bus 64 to bus 1501)\n    target_line_idx = None\n    original_capacity = None\n    for i, br in enumerate(data['branch']):\n        if (int(br[0]) == 64 and int(br[1]) == 1501) or (int(br[0]) == 1501 and int(br[1]) == 64):\n            target_line_idx = i\n            original_capacity = br[5]\n            print(f\"\\nTarget line: Bus {int(br[0])} -> Bus {int(br[1])}\")\n            print(f\"Original capacity: {original_capacity} MW\")\n            break\n\n    # Run base case\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n    print(\"Running BASE CASE scenario...\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n    base_result = run_dc_opf_with_reserves(data)\n    print(f\"Base case status: {base_result['status']}\")\n    print(f\"Base case total cost: ${base_result['total_cost']:,.2f}/hour\")\n    print(f\"Base case reserve MCP: ${base_result['reserve_mcp']:.4f}/MWh\")\n    print(f\"Number of binding lines: {len(base_result['binding_lines'])}\")\n\n    # Check if target line is binding\n    target_binding_base = False\n    for bl in base_result['binding_lines']:\n        if (bl['from'] == 64 and bl['to'] == 1501) or (bl['from'] == 1501 and bl['to'] == 64):\n            target_binding_base = True\n            print(f\"Target line 64-1501 IS BINDING in base case: {bl}\")\n            break\n    if not target_binding_base:\n        print(\"Target line 64-1501 is NOT binding in base case\")\n\n    # Run counterfactual with 20% increased capacity\n    new_capacity = original_capacity * 1.20\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n    print(f\"Running COUNTERFACTUAL scenario (capacity: {original_capacity} -> {new_capacity} MW)...\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n    cf_result = run_dc_opf_with_reserves(data, line_capacity_override={(64, 1501): new_capacity})\n    print(f\"Counterfactual status: {cf_result['status']}\")\n    print(f\"Counterfactual total cost: ${cf_result['total_cost']:,.2f}/hour\")\n    print(f\"Counterfactual reserve MCP: ${cf_result['reserve_mcp']:.4f}/MWh\")\n    print(f\"Number of binding lines: {len(cf_result['binding_lines'])}\")\n\n    # Check if target line is binding in counterfactual\n    target_binding_cf = False\n    for bl in cf_result['binding_lines']:\n        if (bl['from'] == 64 and bl['to'] == 1501) or (bl['from'] == 1501 and bl['to'] == 64):\n            target_binding_cf = True\n            print(f\"Target line 64-1501 IS BINDING in counterfactual: {bl}\")\n            break\n    if not target_binding_cf:\n        print(\"Target line 64-1501 is NOT binding in counterfactual\")\n\n    # Impact analysis\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n    print(\"IMPACT ANALYSIS\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n\n    cost_reduction = base_result['total_cost'] - cf_result['total_cost']\n    print(f\"Cost reduction: ${cost_reduction:,.2f}/hour\")\n\n    # Find buses with largest LMP drop\n    base_lmps = {item['bus']: item['lmp_dollars_per_MWh'] for item in base_result['lmp_by_bus']}\n    cf_lmps = {item['bus']: item['lmp_dollars_per_MWh'] for item in cf_result['lmp_by_bus']}\n\n    lmp_changes = []\n    for bus in base_lmps:\n        delta = cf_lmps[bus] - base_lmps[bus]\n        lmp_changes.append({\n            'bus': bus,\n            'base_lmp': base_lmps[bus],\n            'cf_lmp': cf_lmps[bus],\n            'delta': delta\n        })\n\n    # Sort by delta (most negative = largest drop)\n    lmp_changes.sort(key=lambda x: x['delta'])\n    top_3_drops = lmp_changes[:3]\n\n    print(\"\\nTop 3 buses with largest LMP drop:\")\n    for item in top_3_drops:\n        print(f\"  Bus {item['bus']}: {item['base_lmp']:.4f} -> {item['cf_lmp']:.4f} (delta: {item['delta']:.4f})\")\n\n    congestion_relieved = not target_binding_cf\n    print(f\"\\nCongestion relieved: {congestion_relieved}\")\n\n    # Build report\n    report = {\n        \"base_case\": {\n            \"total_cost_dollars_per_hour\": round(base_result['total_cost'], 2),\n            \"lmp_by_bus\": base_result['lmp_by_bus'],\n            \"reserve_mcp_dollars_per_MWh\": round(base_result['reserve_mcp'], 6),\n            \"binding_lines\": base_result['binding_lines']\n        },\n        \"counterfactual\": {\n            \"total_cost_dollars_per_hour\": round(cf_result['total_cost'], 2),\n            \"lmp_by_bus\": cf_result['lmp_by_bus'],\n            \"reserve_mcp_dollars_per_MWh\": round(cf_result['reserve_mcp'], 6),\n            \"binding_lines\": cf_result['binding_lines']\n        },\n        \"impact_analysis\": {\n            \"cost_reduction_dollars_per_hour\": round(cost_reduction, 2),\n            \"buses_with_largest_lmp_drop\": [\n                {\n                    \"bus\": item['bus'],\n                    \"base_lmp\": round(item['base_lmp'], 6),\n                    \"cf_lmp\": round(item['cf_lmp'], 6),\n                    \"delta\": round(item['delta'], 6)\n                }\n                for item in top_3_drops\n            ],\n            \"congestion_relieved\": congestion_relieved\n        }\n    }\n\n    # {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_vrtx_01M8tH9n84MZ39pgxascZdVa","type":"tool_result","content":"File created successfully at: /root/dc_opf_analysis.py"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"e611fd13-74ca-4acc-9e36-98ff693673eb","uuid":"7df84307-f4bc-4ca1-bcb5-90a648967496","tool_use_result":{"type":"create","filePath":"/root/dc_opf_analysis.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nDC-OPF with Reserve Co-optimization for Market Analysis\nCompares base case with counterfactual scenario (20% increased line capacity on bus 64-1501)\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nimport cvxpy as cp\n\ndef load_network(filepath):\n    \"\"\"Load MATPOWER-format network from JSON\"\"\"\n    with open(filepath, 'r') as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n    return data\n\ndef run_dc_opf_with_reserves(data, line_capacity_override=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run DC-OPF with reserve co-optimization.\n\n    Args:\n        data: Network data dictionary\n        line_capacity_override: Dict of {(from_bus, to_bus): new_capacity} to override\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with solution results including LMPs and reserve MCP\n    \"\"\"\n    baseMVA = data['baseMVA']\n    buses = np.array(data['bus'])\n    gens = np.array(data['gen'])\n    branches = np.array(data['branch'])\n    gencosts = np.array(data['gencost'])\n    reserve_capacities = np.array(data['reserve_capacity'])\n    reserve_requirement = data['reserve_requirement']\n\n    n_bus = len(buses)\n    n_gen = len(gens)\n    n_branch = len(branches)\n\n    # Create bus number to index mapping\n    bus_num_to_idx = {int(buses[i, 0]): i for i in range(n_bus)}\n\n    # Find slack bus (type = 3)\n    slack_idx = None\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        if buses[i, 1] == 3:\n            slack_idx = i\n            break\n    if slack_idx is None:\n        slack_idx = 0  # Default to first bus\n\n    # Get generator bus indices\n    gen_bus_indices = [bus_num_to_idx[int(gens[g, 0])] for g in range(n_gen)]\n\n    # Get generator parameters (only in-service generators)\n    gen_status = gens[:, 7]\n    gen_pmax = gens[:, 8]  # MW\n    gen_pmin = gens[:, 9]  # MW\n\n    # Get load at each bus (column 2 is PD in MW)\n    bus_load = buses[:, 2].copy()  # MW\n\n    # Build susceptance matrix B\n    B = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus))\n    branch_susceptances = []\n    branch_from_idx = []\n    branch_to_idx = []\n    branch_limits = []\n    branch_status = []\n\n    for i, br in enumerate(branches):\n        f_bus = int(br[0])\n        t_bus = int(br[1])\n        x = br[3]  # Reactance\n        rate_a = br[5]  # MW limit\n        status = br[10]\n\n        f = bus_num_to_idx[f_bus]\n        t = bus_num_to_idx[t_bus]\n\n        branch_from_idx.append(f)\n        branch_to_idx.append(t)\n        branch_status.append(status)\n\n        # Check for line capacity override\n        if line_capacity_override is not None:\n            if (f_bus, t_bus) in line_capacity_override:\n                rate_a = line_capacity_override[(f_bus, t_bus)]\n            elif (t_bus, f_bus) in line_capacity_override:\n                rate_a = line_capacity_override[(t_bus, f_bus)]\n\n        branch_limits.append(rate_a)\n\n        if x != 0 and status == 1:\n            b = 1.0 / x\n            B[f, f] += b\n            B[t, t] += b\n            B[f, t] -= b\n            B[t, f] -= b\n            branch_susceptances.append(b)\n        else:\n            branch_susceptances.append(0.0)\n\n    branch_susceptances = np.array(branch_susceptances)\n    branch_from_idx = np.array(branch_from_idx)\n    branch_to_idx = np.array(branch_to_idx)\n    branch_limits = np.array(branch_limits)\n    branch_status = np.array(branch_status)\n\n    # Decision variables\n    Pg = cp.Variable(n_gen, name=\"Pg\")  # Generator real power output (MW)\n    Rg = cp.Variable(n_gen, name=\"Rg\")  # Generator reserve provision (MW)\n    theta = cp.Variable(n_bus, name=\"theta\")  # Bus voltage angles (radians)\n\n    # Cost function: polynomial cost (MODEL=2, NCOST=3 means c2*Pg^2 + c1*Pg + c0)\n    # gencost format: [MODEL, STARTUP, SHUTDOWN, NCOST, c2, c1, c0]\n    total_cost = 0\n    for g in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[g] == 1:\n            c2 = gencosts[g, 4]\n            c1 = gencosts[g, 5]\n            c0 = gencosts[g, 6]\n            # Quadratic cost: c2*Pg^2 + c1*Pg + c0\n            total_cost += c2 * cp.square(Pg[g]) + c1 * Pg[g] + c0\n\n    # Constraints\n    constraints = []\n\n    # 1. Slack bus angle reference\n    constraints.append(theta[slack_idx] == 0)\n\n    # 2. Generator limits (Pmin <= Pg <= Pmax - Rg)\n    for g in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[g] == 1:\n            constraints.append(Pg[g] >= gen_pmin[g])\n            constraints.append(Pg[g] + Rg[g] <= gen_pmax[g])\n            constraints.append(Rg[g] >= 0)\n            constraints.append(Rg[g] <= reserve_capacities[g])\n        else:\n            constraints.append(Pg[g] == 0)\n            constraints.append(Rg[g] == 0)\n\n    # 3. Power balance at each bus: sum(Pg at bus) - Pd = sum(B[i,j] * theta[j])\n    # Build injection vector\n    Pnet = np.zeros(n_bus)  # Will be modified by Pg\n\n    # For DC power flow: P_injection = B @ theta\n    # At each bus: Pg - Pd = sum over j of B[i,j]*theta[j]\n\n    # Create generation at bus matrix (n_bus x n_gen)\n    gen_at_bus = np.zeros((n_bus, n_gen))\n    for g in range(n_gen):\n        gen_at_bus[gen_bus_indices[g], g] = 1.0\n\n    # Power balance constraint: gen_at_bus @ Pg - Pd = B @ theta\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        gen_injection = gen_at_bus[i, :] @ Pg\n        constraints.append(gen_injection - bus_load[i] == B[i, :] @ theta * baseMVA)\n\n    # 4. Line flow limits: |b * (theta_f - theta_t) * baseMVA| <= rate\n    for i in range(n_branch):\n        if branch_status[i] == 1 and branch_limits[i] > 0:\n            f = branch_from_idx[i]\n            t = branch_to_idx[i]\n            b = branch_susceptances[i]\n            flow = b * (theta[f] - theta[t]) * baseMVA\n            constraints.append(flow <= branch_limits[i])\n            constraints.append(flow >= -branch_limits[i])\n\n    # 5. Reserve requirement: sum(Rg) >= reserve_requirement\n    reserve_constraint = cp.sum(Rg) >= reserve_requirement\n    constraints.append(reserve_constraint)\n\n    # Solve the optimization problem\n    problem = cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(total_cost), constraints)\n    problem.solve(solver=cp.ECOS, verbose=False)\n\n    if problem.status not in ['optimal', 'optimal_inaccurate']:\n        raise RuntimeError(f\"Optimization failed with status: {problem.status}\")\n\n    # Extract results\n    Pg_opt = Pg.value\n    Rg_opt = Rg.value\n    theta_opt = theta.value\n\n    # Calculate LMPs from power balance constraint duals\n    # LMP at bus i = dual of power balance constraint at bus i\n    lmps = []\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        # The dual value of the power balance equality constraint\n        dual = constraints[1 + 4*n_gen + i].dual_value\n        if dual is not None:\n            lmp = -dual  # Negative because of constraint formulation\n        else:\n            lmp = 0.0\n        lmps.append(lmp)\n    lmps = np.array(lmps)\n\n    # Calculate reserve MCP from reserve requirement constraint dual\n    reserve_constraint_idx = 1 + 4*n_gen + n_bus + 2*sum(1 for i in range(n_branch) if branch_status[i] == 1 and branch_limits[i] > 0)\n    reserve_mcp = constraints[reserve_constraint_idx].dual_value\n    if reserve_mcp is None:\n        reserve_mcp = 0.0\n    else:\n        reserve_mcp = float(reserve_mcp)\n\n    # Calculate line flows and identify binding lines\n    line_flows = []\n    binding_lines = []\n    for i in range(n_branch):\n        if branch_status[i] == 1:\n            f = branch_from_idx[i]\n            t = branch_to_idx[i]\n            b = branch_susceptances[i]\n            flow = b * (theta_opt[f] - theta_opt[t]) * baseMVA\n            line_flows.append(flow)\n\n            loading = abs(flow) / branch_limits[i] * 100 if branch_limits[i] > 0 else 0\n            if loading >= 99.0:\n                f_bus = int(branches[i, 0])\n                t_bus = int(branches[i, 1])\n                binding_lines.append({\n                    'from': f_bus,\n                    'to': t_bus,\n                    'flow_MW': round(abs(flow), 2),\n                    'limit_MW': round(branch_limits[i], 2),\n                    'loading_pct': round(loading, 2)\n                })\n        else:\n            line_flows.append(0.0)\n\n    # Build LMP by bus result\n    lmp_by_bus = []\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        bus_num = int(buses[i, 0])\n        lmp_by_bus.append({\n            'bus': bus_num,\n            'lmp_dollars_per_MWh': round(lmps[i], 6)\n        })\n\n    return {\n        'total_cost': problem.value,\n        'lmp_by_bus': lmp_by_bus,\n        'lmps': lmps,\n        'reserve_mcp': reserve_mcp,\n        'binding_lines': binding_lines,\n        'Pg': Pg_opt,\n        'Rg': Rg_opt,\n        'theta': theta_opt,\n        'buses': buses,\n        'status': problem.status\n    }\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading network data...\")\n    data = load_network('/root/network.json')\n\n    print(f\"Network: {data['name']}\")\n    print(f\"Buses: {len(data['bus'])}, Generators: {len(data['gen'])}, Branches: {len(data['branch'])}\")\n    print(f\"Reserve requirement: {data['reserve_requirement']} MW\")\n\n    # Find the target line (bus 64 to bus 1501)\n    target_line_idx = None\n    original_capacity = None\n    for i, br in enumerate(data['branch']):\n        if (int(br[0]) == 64 and int(br[1]) == 1501) or (int(br[0]) == 1501 and int(br[1]) == 64):\n            target_line_idx = i\n            original_capacity = br[5]\n            print(f\"\\nTarget line: Bus {int(br[0])} -> Bus {int(br[1])}\")\n            print(f\"Original capacity: {original_capacity} MW\")\n            break\n\n    # Run counterfactual with 20% increased capacity\n    new_capacity = original_capacity * 1.20\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n    print(f\"Running COUNTERFACTUAL scenario (capacity: {original_capacity} -> {new_capacity} MW)...\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n    cf_result = run_dc_opf_with_reserves(data, line_capacity_override={(64, 1501): new_capacity})\n    print(f\"Counterfactual status: {cf_result['status']}\")\n    print(f\"Counterfactual total cost: ${cf_result['total_cost']:,.2f}/hour\")\n    print(f\"Counterfactual reserve MCP: ${cf_result['reserve_mcp']:.4f}/MWh\")\n    print(f\"Number of binding lines: {len(cf_result['binding_lines'])}\")\n\n    # Check if target line is binding in counterfactual\n    target_binding_cf = False\n    for bl in cf_result['binding_lines']:\n        if (bl['from'] == 64 and bl['to'] == 1501) or (bl['from'] == 1501 and bl['to'] == 64):\n            target_binding_cf = True\n            print(f\"Target line 64-1501 IS BINDING in counterfactual: {bl}\")\n            break\n    if not target_binding_cf:\n        print(\"Target line 64-1501 is NOT binding in counterfactual\")\n\n    # {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-5-20251101","id":"msg_vrtx_0117E54ESN41krmstEk8Zswj","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_01JqciNYYv9AgZsajZfckuvr","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/dc_opf_analysis.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nDC-OPF with Reserve Co-optimization for Market Analysis\nCompares base case with counterfactual scenario (20% increased line capacity on bus 64-1501)\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nimport cvxpy as cp\n\ndef load_network(filepath):\n    \"\"\"Load MATPOWER-format network from JSON\"\"\"\n    with open(filepath, 'r') as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n    return data\n\ndef run_dc_opf_with_reserves(data, line_capacity_override=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run DC-OPF with reserve co-optimization.\n\n    Args:\n        data: Network data dictionary\n        line_capacity_override: Dict of {(from_bus, to_bus): new_capacity} to override\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with solution results including LMPs and reserve MCP\n    \"\"\"\n    baseMVA = data['baseMVA']\n    buses = np.array(data['bus'])\n    gens = np.array(data['gen'])\n    branches = np.array(data['branch'])\n    gencosts = np.array(data['gencost'])\n    reserve_capacities = np.array(data['reserve_capacity'])\n    reserve_requirement = data['reserve_requirement']\n\n    n_bus = len(buses)\n    n_gen = len(gens)\n    n_branch = len(branches)\n\n    # Create bus number to index mapping\n    bus_num_to_idx = {int(buses[i, 0]): i for i in range(n_bus)}\n\n    # Find slack bus (type = 3)\n    slack_idx = None\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        if buses[i, 1] == 3:\n            slack_idx = i\n            break\n    if slack_idx is None:\n        slack_idx = 0  # Default to first bus\n\n    # Get generator bus indices\n    gen_bus_indices = [bus_num_to_idx[int(gens[g, 0])] for g in range(n_gen)]\n\n    # Get generator parameters (only in-service generators)\n    gen_status = gens[:, 7]\n    gen_pmax = gens[:, 8]  # MW\n    gen_pmin = gens[:, 9]  # MW\n\n    # Get load at each bus (column 2 is PD in MW)\n    bus_load = buses[:, 2].copy()  # MW\n\n    # Build susceptance matrix B and branch data\n    B = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus))\n    branch_susceptances = []\n    branch_from_idx = []\n    branch_to_idx = []\n    branch_limits = []\n    branch_status = []\n    branch_from_bus = []\n    branch_to_bus = []\n\n    for i, br in enumerate(branches):\n        f_bus = int(br[0])\n        t_bus = int(br[1])\n        x = br[3]  # Reactance\n        rate_a = br[5]  # MW limit\n        status = br[10]\n\n        f = bus_num_to_idx[f_bus]\n        t = bus_num_to_idx[t_bus]\n\n        branch_from_idx.append(f)\n        branch_to_idx.append(t)\n        branch_status.append(status)\n        branch_from_bus.append(f_bus)\n        branch_to_bus.append(t_bus)\n\n        # Check for line capacity override\n        if line_capacity_override is not None:\n            if (f_bus, t_bus) in line_capacity_override:\n                rate_a = line_capacity_override[(f_bus, t_bus)]\n            elif (t_bus, f_bus) in line_capacity_override:\n                rate_a = line_capacity_override[(t_bus, f_bus)]\n\n        branch_limits.append(rate_a)\n\n        if x != 0 and status == 1:\n            b = 1.0 / x\n            B[f, f] += b\n            B[t, t] += b\n            B[f, t] -= b\n            B[t, f] -= b\n            branch_susceptances.append(b)\n        else:\n            branch_susceptances.append(0.0)\n\n    branch_susceptances = np.array(branch_susceptances)\n    branch_from_idx = np.array(branch_from_idx)\n    branch_to_idx = np.array(branch_to_idx)\n    branch_limits = np.array(branch_limits)\n    branch_status = np.array(branch_status)\n\n    # Decision variables\n    Pg = cp.Variable(n_gen, name=\"Pg\")  # Generator real power output (MW)\n    Rg = cp.Variable(n_gen, name=\"Rg\")  # Generator reserve provision (MW)\n    theta = cp.Variable(n_bus, name=\"theta\")  # Bus voltage angles (radians)\n\n    # Cost function: polynomial cost (MODEL=2, NCOST=3 means c2*Pg^2 + c1*Pg + c0)\n    # gencost format: [MODEL, STARTUP, SHUTDOWN, NCOST, c2, c1, c0]\n    total_cost = 0\n    for g in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[g] == 1:\n            c2 = gencosts[g, 4]\n            c1 = gencosts[g, 5]\n            c0 = gencosts[g, 6]\n            # Quadratic cost: c2*Pg^2 + c1*Pg + c0\n            total_cost += c2 * cp.square(Pg[g]) + c1 * Pg[g] + c0\n\n    # Constraints - store references for dual extraction\n    constraints = []\n\n    # 1. Slack bus angle reference\n    slack_constraint = theta[slack_idx] == 0\n    constraints.append(slack_constraint)\n\n    # 2. Generator limits\n    gen_constraints = []\n    for g in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[g] == 1:\n            constraints.append(Pg[g] >= gen_pmin[g])\n            constraints.append(Pg[g] + Rg[g] <= gen_pmax[g])\n            constraints.append(Rg[g] >= 0)\n            constraints.append(Rg[g] <= reserve_capacities[g])\n        else:\n            constraints.append(Pg[g] == 0)\n            constraints.append(Rg[g] == 0)\n\n    # 3. Power balance at each bus - store these separately for LMP extraction\n    # At each bus: Pg - Pd = B @ theta * baseMVA\n    gen_at_bus = np.zeros((n_bus, n_gen))\n    for g in range(n_gen):\n        gen_at_bus[gen_bus_indices[g], g] = 1.0\n\n    power_balance_constraints = []\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        gen_injection = gen_at_bus[i, :] @ Pg\n        pbc = gen_injection - bus_load[i] == B[i, :] @ theta * baseMVA\n        power_balance_constraints.append(pbc)\n        constraints.append(pbc)\n\n    # 4. Line flow limits\n    line_flow_constraints = []\n    active_branch_indices = []\n    for i in range(n_branch):\n        if branch_status[i] == 1 and branch_limits[i] > 0:\n            f = branch_from_idx[i]\n            t = branch_to_idx[i]\n            b = branch_susceptances[i]\n            flow = b * (theta[f] - theta[t]) * baseMVA\n            c1 = flow <= branch_limits[i]\n            c2 = flow >= -branch_limits[i]\n            line_flow_constraints.append((c1, c2, i))\n            active_branch_indices.append(i)\n            constraints.append(c1)\n            constraints.append(c2)\n\n    # 5. Reserve requirement\n    reserve_constraint = cp.sum(Rg) >= reserve_requirement\n    constraints.append(reserve_constraint)\n\n    # Solve the optimization problem with SCS (better for large problems)\n    problem = cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(total_cost), constraints)\n\n    # Try multiple solvers\n    solved = False\n    for solver in [cp.SCS, cp.OSQP, cp.CLARABEL]:\n        try:\n            if solver == cp.SCS:\n                problem.solve(solver=solver, verbose=False, max_iters=10000, eps=1e-5)\n            elif solver == cp.OSQP:\n                problem.solve(solver=solver, verbose=False, eps_abs=1e-5, eps_rel=1e-5, max_iter=10000)\n            else:\n                problem.solve(solver=solver, verbose=False)\n\n            if problem.status in ['optimal', 'optimal_inaccurate']:\n                solved = True\n                break\n        except Exception as e:\n            continue\n\n    if not solved:\n        raise RuntimeError(f\"Optimization failed with status: {problem.status}\")\n\n    # Create bus number to index mapping\n    bus_num_to_idx = {int(buses[i, 0]): i for i in range(n_bus)}\n\n    # Find slack bus (type = 3)\n    slack_idx = None\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        if buses[i, 1] == 3:\n            slack_idx = i\n            break\n    if slack_idx is None:\n        slack_idx = 0  # Default to first bus\n\n    # Get generator bus indices\n    gen_bus_indices = [bus_num_to_idx[int(gens[g, 0])] for g in range(n_gen)]\n\n    # Get generator parameters (only in-service generators)\n    gen_status = gens[:, 7]\n    gen_pmax = gens[:, 8]  # MW\n    gen_pmin = gens[:, 9]  # MW\n\n    # Get load at each bus (column 2 is PD in MW)\n    bus_load = buses[:, 2].copy()  # MW\n\n    # Build susceptance matrix B and branch data\n    B = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus))\n    branch_susceptances = []\n    branch_from_idx = []\n    branch_to_idx = []\n    branch_limits = []\n    branch_status = []\n    branch_from_bus = []\n    branch_to_bus = []\n\n    for i, br in enumerate(branches):\n        f_bus = int(br[0])\n        t_bus = int(br[1])\n        x = br[3]  # Reactance\n        rate_a = br[5]  # MW limit\n        status = br[10]\n\n        f = bus_num_to_idx[f_bus]\n        t = bus_num_to_idx[t_bus]\n\n        branch_from_idx.append(f)\n        branch_to_idx.append(t)\n        branch_status.append(status)\n        branch_from_bus.append(f_bus)\n        branch_to_bus.append(t_bus)\n\n        # Check for line capacity override\n        if line_capacity_override is not None:\n            if (f_bus, t_bus) in line_capacity_override:\n                rate_a = line_capacity_override[(f_bus, t_bus)]\n            elif (t_bus, f_bus) in line_capacity_override:\n                rate_a = line_capacity_override[(t_bus, f_bus)]\n\n        branch_limits.append(rate_a)\n\n        if x != 0 and status == 1:\n            b = 1.0 / x\n            B[f, f] += b\n            B[t, t] += b\n            B[f, t] -= b\n            B[t, f] -= b\n            branch_susceptances.append(b)\n        else:\n            branch_susceptances.append(0.0)\n\n    branch_susceptances = np.array(branch_susceptances)\n    branch_from_idx = np.array(branch_from_idx)\n    branch_to_idx = np.array(branch_to_idx)\n    branch_limits = np.array(branch_limits)\n    branch_status = np.array(branch_status)\n\n    # Decision variables\n    Pg = cp.Variable(n_gen, name=\"Pg\")  # Generator real power output (MW)\n    Rg = cp.Variable(n_gen, name=\"Rg\")  # Generator reserve provision (MW)\n    theta = cp.Variable(n_bus, name=\"theta\")  # Bus voltage angles (radians)\n\n    # Cost function: polynomial cost (MODEL=2, NCOST=3 means c2*Pg^2 + c1*Pg + c0)\n    # gencost format: [MODEL, STARTUP, SHUTDOWN, NCOST, c2, c1, c0]\n    total_cost = 0\n    for g in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[g] == 1:\n            c2 = gencosts[g, 4]\n            c1 = gencosts[g, 5]\n            c0 = gencosts[g, 6]\n            # Quadratic cost: c2*Pg^2 + c1*Pg + c0\n            total_cost += c2 * cp.square(Pg[g]) + c1 * Pg[g] + c0\n\n    # Constraints - store references for dual extraction\n    constraints = []\n\n    # 1. Slack bus angle reference\n    slack_constraint = theta[slack_idx] == 0\n    constraints.append(slack_constraint)\n\n    # 2. Generator limits\n    gen_constraints = []\n    for g in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[g] == 1:\n            constraints.append(Pg[g] >= gen_pmin[g])\n            constraints.append(Pg[g] + Rg[g] <= gen_pmax[g])\n            constraints.append(Rg[g] >= 0)\n            constraints.append(Rg[g] <= reserve_capacities[g])\n        else:\n            constraints.append(Pg[g] == 0)\n            constraints.append(Rg[g] == 0)\n\n    # 3. Power balance at each bus - store these separately for LMP extraction\n    # At each bus: Pg - Pd = B @ theta * baseMVA\n    gen_at_bus = np.zeros((n_bus, n_gen))\n    for g in range(n_gen):\n        gen_at_bus[gen_bus_indices[g], g] = 1.0\n\n    power_balance_constraints = []\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        gen_injection = gen_at_bus[i, :] @ Pg\n        pbc = gen_injection - bus_load[i] == B[i, :] @ theta * baseMVA\n        power_balance_constraints.append(pbc)\n        constraints.append(pbc)\n\n    # 4. Line flow limits\n    line_flow_constraints = []\n    active_branch_indices = []\n    for i in range(n_branch):\n        if branch_status[i] == 1 and branch_limits[i] > 0:\n            f = branch_from_idx[i]\n            t = branch_to_idx[i]\n            b = branch_susceptances[i]\n            flow = b * (theta[f] - theta[t]) * baseMVA\n            c1 = flow <= branch_limits[i]\n            c2 = flow >= -branch_limits[i]\n            line_flow_constraints.append((c1, c2, i))\n            active_branch_indices.append(i)\n            constraints.append(c1)\n            constraints.append(c2)\n\n    # 5. Reserve requirement\n    reserve_constraint = cp.sum(Rg) >= reserve_requirement\n    constraints.append(reserve_constraint)\n\n    # Solve the optimization problem with SCS (better for large problems)\n    problem = cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(total_cost), constraints)\n\n    # Try multiple solvers\n    solved = False\n    for solver in [cp.SCS, cp.OSQP, cp.CLARABEL]:\n        try:\n            if solver == cp.SCS:\n                problem.solve(solver=solver, verbose=False, max_iters=10000, eps=1e-5)\n            elif solver == cp.OSQP:\n                problem.solve(solver=solver, verbose=False, eps_abs=1e-5, eps_rel=1e-5, max_iter=10000)\n            else:\n                problem.solve(solver=solver, verbose=False)\n\n            if problem.status in ['optimal', 'optimal_inaccurate']:\n                solved = True\n                break\n        except Exception as e:\n            continue\n\n    if not solved:\n        raise RuntimeError(f\"Optimization failed with status: {problem.status}\")\n\n    # Extract results\n    Pg_opt = Pg.value\n    Rg_opt = Rg.value\n    theta_opt = theta.value\n\n    # Extract LMPs from power balance constraint duals\n    lmps = []\n    for i, pbc in enumerate(power_balance_constraints):\n        dual = pbc.dual_value\n        if dual is not None:\n            # LMP = marginal cost of supplying one more MW at this bus\n            # Sign convention: positive dual means higher cost to serve load\n            lmp = float(dual)\n        else:\n            lmp = 0.0\n        lmps.append(lmp)\n    lmps = np.array(lmps)\n\n    # Extract reserve MCP from reserve requirement constraint dual\n    reserve_mcp = reserve_constraint.dual_value\n    if reserve_mcp is None:\n        reserve_mcp = 0.0\n    else:\n        reserve_mcp = float(reserve_mcp)\n\n    # Calculate line flows and identify binding lines\n    line_flows = []\n    binding_lines = []\n    for i in range(n_branch):\n        if branch_status[i] == 1:\n            f = branch_from_idx[i]\n            t = branch_to_idx[i]\n            b = branch_susceptances[i]\n            flow = b * (theta_opt[f] - theta_opt[t]) * baseMVA\n            line_flows.append(flow)\n\n            loading = abs(flow) / branch_limits[i] * 100 if branch_limits[i] > 0 else 0\n            if loading >= 99.0:\n                f_bus = branch_from_bus[i]\n                t_bus = branch_to_bus[i]\n                binding_lines.append({\n                    'from': f_bus,\n                    'to': t_bus,\n                    'flow_MW': round(abs(flow), 2),\n                    'limit_MW': round(branch_limits[i], 2),\n                    'loading_pct': round(loading, 2)\n                })\n        else:\n            line_flows.append(0.0)\n\n    # Build LMP by bus result\n    lmp_by_bus = []\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        bus_num = int(buses[i, 0])\n        lmp_by_bus.append({\n            'bus': bus_num,\n            'lmp_dollars_per_MWh': round(lmps[i], 6)\n        })\n\n    return {\n        'total_cost': problem.value,\n        'lmp_by_bus': lmp_by_bus,\n        'lmps': lmps,\n        'reserve_mcp': reserve_mcp,\n        'binding_lines': binding_lines,\n        'Pg': Pg_opt,\n        'Rg': Rg_opt,\n        'theta': theta_opt,\n        'buses': buses,\n        'status': problem.status\n    }\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading network data...\")\n    data = load_network('/root/network.json')\n\n    print(f\"Network: {data['name']}\")\n    print(f\"Buses: {len(data['bus'])}, Generators: {len(data['gen'])}, Branches: {len(data['branch'])}\")\n    print(f\"Reserve requirement: {data['reserve_requirement']} MW\")\n\n    # Find the target line (bus 64 to bus 1501)\n    target_line_idx = None\n    original_capacity = None\n    for i, br in enumerate(data['branch']):\n        if (int(br[0]) == 64 and int(br[1]) == 1501) or (int(br[0]) == 1501 and int(br[1]) == 64):\n            target_line_idx = i\n            original_capacity = br[5]\n            print(f\"\\nTarget line: Bus {int(br[0])} -> Bus {int(br[1])}\")\n            print(f\"Original capacity: {original_capacity} MW\")\n            break\n\n    # Run base case\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n    print(\"Running BASE CASE scenario...\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n    base_result = run_dc_opf_with_reserves(data)\n    print(f\"Base case status: {base_result['status']}\")\n    print(f\"Base case total cost: ${base_result['total_cost']:,.2f}/hour\")\n    print(f\"Base case reserve MCP: ${base_result['reserve_mcp']:.4f}/MWh\")\n    print(f\"Number of binding lines: {len(base_result['binding_lines'])}\")\n\n    # Check if target line is binding\n    target_binding_base = False\n    for bl in base_result['binding_lines']:\n        if (bl['from'] == 64 and bl['to'] == 1501) or (bl['from'] == 1501 and bl['to'] == 64):\n            target_binding_base = True\n            print(f\"Target line 64-1501 IS BINDING in base case: {bl}\")\n            break\n    if not target_binding_base:\n        print(\"Target line 64-1501 is NOT binding in base case\")\n\n    # Run counterfactual with 20% increased capacity\n    new_capacity = original_capacity * 1.20\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n    print(f\"Running COUNTERFACTUAL scenario (capacity: {original_capacity} -> {new_capacity} MW)...\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n    cf_result = run_dc_opf_with_reserves(data, line_capacity_override={(64, 1501): new_capacity})\n    print(f\"Counterfactual status: {cf_result['status']}\")\n    print(f\"Counterfactual total cost: ${cf_result['total_cost']:,.2f}/hour\")\n    print(f\"Counterfactual reserve MCP: ${cf_result['reserve_mcp']:.4f}/MWh\")\n    print(f\"Number of binding lines: {len(cf_result['binding_lines'])}\")\n\n    # Check if target line is binding in counterfactual\n    target_binding_cf = False\n    for bl in cf_result['binding_lines']:\n        if (bl['from'] == 64 and bl['to'] == 1501) or (bl['from'] == 1501 and bl['to'] == 64):\n            target_binding_cf = True\n            print(f\"Target line 64-1501 IS BINDING in counterfactual: {bl}\")\n            break\n    if not target_binding_cf:\n        print(\"Target line 64-1501 is NOT binding in counterfactual\")\n\n    # Impact analysis\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n    print(\"IMPACT ANALYSIS\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n\n    cost_reduction = base_result['total_cost'] - cf_result['total_cost']\n    print(f\"Cost reduction: ${cost_reduction:,.2f}/hour\")\n\n    # Find buses with largest LMP drop\n    base_lmps = {item['bus']: item['lmp_dollars_per_MWh'] for item in base_result['lmp_by_bus']}\n    cf_lmps = {item['bus']: item['lmp_dollars_per_MWh'] for item in cf_result['lmp_by_bus']}\n\n    lmp_changes = []\n    for bus in base_lmps:\n        delta = cf_lmps[bus] - base_lmps[bus]\n        lmp_changes.append({\n            'bus': bus,\n            'base_lmp': base_lmps[bus],\n            'cf_lmp': cf_lmps[bus],\n            'delta': delta\n        })\n\n    # Sort by delta (most negative = largest drop)\n    lmp_changes.sort(key=lambda x: x['delta'])\n    top_3_drops = lmp_changes[:3]\n\n    print(\"\\nTop 3 buses with largest LMP drop:\")\n    for item in top_3_drops:\n        print(f\"  Bus {item['bus']}: {item['base_lmp']:.4f} -> {item['cf_lmp']:.4f} (delta: {item['delta']:.4f})\")\n\n    congestion_relieved = not target_binding_cf\n    print(f\"\\nCongestion relieved: {congestion_relieved}\")\n\n    # Build report\n    report = {\n        \"base_case\": {\n            \"total_cost_dollars_per_hour\": round(base_result['total_cost'], 2),\n            \"lmp_by_bus\": base_result['lmp_by_bus'],\n            \"reserve_mcp_dollars_per_MWh\": round(base_result['reserve_mcp'], 6),\n            \"binding_lines\": base_result['binding_lines']\n        },\n        \"counterfactual\": {\n            \"total_cost_dollars_per_hour\": round(cf_result['total_cost'], 2),\n            \"lmp_by_bus\": cf_result['lmp_by_bus'],\n            \"reserve_mcp_dollars_per_MWh\": round(cf_result['reserve_mcp'], 6),\n            \"binding_lines\": cf_result['binding_lines']\n        },\n        \"impact_analysis\": {\n            \"cost_reduction_dollars_per_hour\": round(cost_reduction, 2),\n            \"buses_with_largest_lmp_drop\": [\n                {\n                    \"bus\": item['bus'],\n                    \"base_lmp\": round(item['base_lmp'], 6),\n                    \"cf_lmp\": round(item['cf_lmp'], 6),\n                    \"delta\": round(item['delta'], 6)\n                }\n                for item in top_3_drops\n            ],\n            \"congestion_relieved\": congestion_relieved\n        }\n    }\n\n    # Save report\n    with open('/root/report.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(report, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n    print(\"Report saved to /root/report.json\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n\n    return report\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":60,"oldLines":13,"newStart":60,"newLines":15,"lines":["     # Create bus number to index mapping\n    bus_num_to_idx = {int(buses[i, 0]): i for i in range(n_bus)}\n\n    # Find slack bus (type = 3)\n    slack_idx = None\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        if buses[i, 1] == 3:\n            slack_idx = i\n            break\n    if slack_idx is None:\n        slack_idx = 0  # Default to first bus\n\n    # Get generator bus indices\n    gen_bus_indices = [bus_num_to_idx[int(gens[g, 0])] for g in range(n_gen)]\n\n    # Get generator parameters (only in-service generators)\n    gen_status = gens[:, 7]\n    gen_pmax = gens[:, 8]  # MW\n    gen_pmin = gens[:, 9]  # MW\n\n    # Get load at each bus (column 2 is PD in MW)\n    bus_load = buses[:, 2].copy()  # MW\n\n    # Build susceptance matrix B\n    B = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus))\n    branch_susceptances = []\n    branch_from_idx = []\n    branch_to_idx = []\n    branch_limits = []\n    branch_status = []\n\n    for i, br in enumerate(branches):\n        f_bus = int(br[0])\n        t_bus = int(br[1])\n        x = br[3]  # Reactance\n        rate_a = br[5]  # MW limit\n        status = br[10]\n\n        f = bus_num_to_idx[f_bus]\n        t = bus_num_to_idx[t_bus]\n\n        branch_from_idx.append(f)\n        branch_to_idx.append(t)\n        branch_status.append(status)\n\n        # Check for line capacity override\n        if line_capacity_override is not None:\n            if (f_bus, t_bus) in line_capacity_override:\n                rate_a = line_capacity_override[(f_bus, t_bus)]\n            elif (t_bus, f_bus) in line_capacity_override:\n                rate_a = line_capacity_override[(t_bus, f_bus)]\n\n        branch_limits.append(rate_a)\n\n        if x != 0 and status == 1:\n            b = 1.0 / x\n            B[f, f] += b\n            B[t, t] += b\n            B[f, t] -= b\n            B[t, f] -= b\n            branch_susceptances.append(b)\n        else:\n            branch_susceptances.append(0.0)\n\n    branch_susceptances = np.array(branch_susceptances)\n    branch_from_idx = np.array(branch_from_idx)\n    branch_to_idx = np.array(branch_to_idx)\n    branch_limits = np.array(branch_limits)\n    branch_status = np.array(branch_status)\n\n    # Decision variables\n    Pg = cp.Variable(n_gen, name=\"Pg\")  # Generator real power output (MW)\n    Rg = cp.Variable(n_gen, name=\"Rg\")  # Generator reserve provision (MW)\n    theta = cp.Variable(n_bus, name=\"theta\")  # Bus voltage angles (radians)\n\n    # Cost function: polynomial cost (MODEL=2, NCOST=3 means c2*Pg^2 + c1*Pg + c0)\n    # gencost format: [MODEL, STARTUP, SHUTDOWN, NCOST, c2, c1, c0]\n    total_cost = 0\n    for g in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[g] == 1:\n            c2 = gencosts[g, 4]\n            c1 = gencosts[g, 5]\n            c0 = gencosts[g, 6]\n            # Quadratic cost: c2*Pg^2 + c1*Pg + c0\n            total_cost += c2 * cp.square(Pg[g]) + c1 * Pg[g] + c0\n\n    # Constraints\n    constraints = []\n\n    # 1. Slack bus angle reference\n    constraints.append(theta[slack_idx] == 0)\n\n    # 2. Generator limits (Pmin <= Pg <= Pmax - Rg)\n    for g in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[g] == 1:\n            constraints.append(Pg[g] >= gen_pmin[g])\n            constraints.append(Pg[g] + Rg[g] <= gen_pmax[g])\n            constraints.append(Rg[g] >= 0)\n            constraints.append(Rg[g] <= reserve_capacities[g])\n        else:\n            constraints.append(Pg[g] == 0)\n            constraints.append(Rg[g] == 0)\n\n    # 3. Power balance at each bus: sum(Pg at bus) - Pd = sum(B[i,j] * theta[j])\n    # Build injection vector\n    Pnet = np.zeros(n_bus)  # Will be modified by Pg\n\n    # For DC power flow: P_injection = B @ theta\n    # At each bus: Pg - Pd = sum over j of B[i,j]*theta[j]\n\n    # Create generation at bus matrix (n_bus x n_gen)\n    gen_at_bus = np.zeros((n_bus, n_gen))\n    for g in range(n_gen):\n        gen_at_bus[gen_bus_indices[g], g] = 1.0\n\n    # Power balance constraint: gen_at_bus @ Pg - Pd = B @ theta\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        gen_injection = gen_at_bus[i, :] @ Pg\n        constraints.append(gen_injection - bus_load[i] == B[i, :] @ theta * baseMVA)\n\n    # 4. Line flow limits: |b * (theta_f - theta_t) * baseMVA| <= rate\n    for i in range(n_branch):\n        if branch_status[i] == 1 and branch_limits[i] > 0:\n            f = branch_from_idx[i]\n            t = branch_to_idx[i]\n            b = branch_susceptances[i]\n            flow = b * (theta[f] - theta[t]) * baseMVA\n            constraints.append(flow <= branch_limits[i])\n            constraints.append(flow >= -branch_limits[i])\n\n    # 5. Reserve requirement: sum(Rg) >= reserve_requirement\n    reserve_constraint = cp.sum(Rg) >= reserve_requirement\n    constraints.append(reserve_constraint)\n\n    # Solve the optimization problem\n    problem = cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(total_cost), constraints)\n    problem.solve(solver=cp.ECOS, verbose=False)\n\n    if problem.status not in ['optimal', 'optimal_inaccurate']:\n        raise RuntimeError(f\"Optimization failed with status: {problem.status}\")\n\n    # Extract results\n    Pg_opt = Pg.value\n    Rg_opt = Rg.value\n    theta_opt = theta.value\n\n    # Calculate LMPs from power balance constraint duals\n    # LMP at bus i = dual of power balance constraint at bus i\n    lmps = []\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        # The dual value of the power balance equality constraint\n        dual = constraints[1 + 4*n_gen + i].dual_value\n        if dual is not None:\n            lmp = -dual  # Negative because of constraint formulation\n        else:\n            lmp = 0.0\n        lmps.append(lmp)\n    lmps = np.array(lmps)\n\n    # Calculate reserve MCP from reserve requirement constraint dual\n    reserve_constraint_idx = 1 + 4*n_gen + n_bus + 2*sum(1 for i in range(n_branch) if branch_status[i] == 1 and branch_limits[i] > 0)\n    reserve_mcp = constraints[reserve_constraint_idx].dual_value\n    if reserve_mcp is None:\n        reserve_mcp = 0.0\n    else:\n        reserve_mcp = float(reserve_mcp)\n\n    # Calculate line flows and identify binding lines\n    line_flows = []\n    binding_lines = []\n    for i in range(n_branch):\n        if branch_status[i] == 1:\n            f = branch_from_idx[i]\n            t = branch_to_idx[i]\n            b = branch_susceptances[i]\n            flow = b * (theta_opt[f] - theta_opt[t]) * baseMVA\n            line_flows.append(flow)\n\n            loading = abs(flow) / branch_limits[i] * 100 if branch_limits[i] > 0 else 0\n            if loading >= 99.0:\n                f_bus = int(branches[i, 0])\n                t_bus = int(branches[i, 1])\n                binding_lines.append({\n                    'from': f_bus,\n                    'to': t_bus,\n                    'flow_MW': round(abs(flow), 2),\n                    'limit_MW': round(branch_limits[i], 2),\n                    'loading_pct': round(loading, 2)\n                })\n        else:\n            line_flows.append(0.0)\n\n    # Build LMP by bus result\n    lmp_by_bus = []\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        bus_num = int(buses[i, 0])\n        lmp_by_bus.append({\n            'bus': bus_num,\n            'lmp_dollars_per_MWh': round(lmps[i], 6)\n        })\n\n    return {\n        'total_cost': problem.value,\n        'lmp_by_bus': lmp_by_bus,\n        'lmps': lmps,\n        'reserve_mcp': reserve_mcp,\n        'binding_lines': binding_lines,\n        'Pg': Pg_opt,\n        'Rg': Rg_opt,\n        'theta': theta_opt,\n        'buses': buses,\n        'status': problem.status\n    }\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading network data...\")\n    data = load_network('/root/network.json')\n\n    print(f\"Network: {data['name']}\")\n    print(f\"Buses: {len(data['bus'])}, Generators: {len(data['gen'])}, Branches: {len(data['branch'])}\")\n    print(f\"Reserve requirement: {data['reserve_requirement']} MW\")\n\n    # Find the target line (bus 64 to bus 1501)\n    target_line_idx = None\n    original_capacity = None\n    for i, br in enumerate(data['branch']):\n        if (int(br[0]) == 64 and int(br[1]) == 1501) or (int(br[0]) == 1501 and int(br[1]) == 64):\n            target_line_idx = i\n            original_capacity = br[5]\n            print(f\"\\nTarget line: Bus {int(br[0])} -> Bus {int(br[1])}\")\n            print(f\"Original capacity: {original_capacity} MW\")\n            break\n\n    # Run base case\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n    print(\"Running BASE CASE scenario...\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n    base_result = run_dc_opf_with_reserves(data)\n    print(f\"Base case status: {base_result['status']}\")\n    print(f\"Base case total cost: ${base_result['total_cost']:,.2f}/hour\")\n    print(f\"Base case reserve MCP: ${base_result['reserve_mcp']:.4f}/MWh\")\n    print(f\"Number of binding lines: {len(base_result['binding_lines'])}\")\n\n    # Check if target line is binding\n    target_binding_base = False\n    for bl in base_result['binding_lines']:\n        if (bl['from'] == 64 and bl['to'] == 1501) or (bl['from'] == 1501 and bl['to'] == 64):\n            target_binding_base = True\n            print(f\"Target line 64-1501 IS BINDING in base case: {bl}\")\n            break\n    if not target_binding_base:\n        print(\"Target line 64-1501 is NOT binding in base case\")\n\n    # Run counterfactual with 20% increased capacity\n    new_capacity = original_capacity * 1.20\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n    print(f\"Running COUNTERFACTUAL scenario (capacity: {original_capacity} -> {new_capacity} MW)...\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n    cf_result = run_dc_opf_with_reserves(data, line_capacity_override={(64, 1501): new_capacity})\n    print(f\"Counterfactual status: {cf_result['status']}\")\n    print(f\"Counterfactual total cost: ${cf_result['total_cost']:,.2f}/hour\")\n    print(f\"Counterfactual reserve MCP: ${cf_result['reserve_mcp']:.4f}/MWh\")\n    print(f\"Number of binding lines: {len(cf_result['binding_lines'])}\")\n\n    # Check if target line is binding in counterfactual\n    target_binding_cf = False\n    for bl in cf_result['binding_lines']:\n        if (bl['from'] == 64 and bl['to'] == 1501) or (bl['from'] == 1501 and bl['to'] == 64):\n            target_binding_cf = True\n            print(f\"Target line 64-1501 IS BINDING in counterfactual: {bl}\")\n            break\n    if not target_binding_cf:\n        print(\"Target line 64-1501 is NOT binding in counterfactual\")\n\n    # Impact analysis\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n    print(\"IMPACT ANALYSIS\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n\n    cost_reduction = base_result['total_cost'] - cf_result['total_cost']\n    print(f\"Cost reduction: ${cost_reduction:,.2f}/hour\")\n\n    # Find buses with largest LMP drop\n    base_lmps = {item['bus']: item['lmp_dollars_per_MWh'] for item in base_result['lmp_by_bus']}\n    cf_lmps = {item['bus']: item['lmp_dollars_per_MWh'] for item in cf_result['lmp_by_bus']}\n\n    lmp_changes = []\n    for bus in base_lmps:\n        delta = cf_lmps[bus] - base_lmps[bus]\n        lmp_changes.append({\n            'bus': bus,\n            'base_lmp': base_lmps[bus],\n            'cf_lmp': cf_lmps[bus],\n            'delta': delta\n        })\n\n    # Sort by delta (most negative = largest drop)\n    lmp_changes.sort(key=lambda x: x['delta'])\n    top_3_drops = lmp_changes[:3]\n\n    print(\"\\nTop 3 buses with largest LMP drop:\")\n    for item in top_3_drops:\n        print(f\"  Bus {item['bus']}: {item['base_lmp']:.4f} -> {item['cf_lmp']:.4f} (delta: {item['delta']:.4f})\")\n\n    congestion_relieved = not target_binding_cf\n    print(f\"\\nCongestion relieved: {congestion_relieved}\")\n\n    # Build report\n    report = {\n        \"base_case\": {\n            \"total_cost_dollars_per_hour\": round(base_result['total_cost'], 2),\n            \"lmp_by_bus\": base_result['lmp_by_bus'],\n            \"reserve_mcp_dollars_per_MWh\": round(base_result['reserve_mcp'], 6),\n            \"binding_lines\": base_result['binding_lines']\n        },\n        \"counterfactual\": {\n            \"total_cost_dollars_per_hour\": round(cf_result['total_cost'], 2),\n            \"lmp_by_bus\": cf_result['lmp_by_bus'],\n            \"reserve_mcp_dollars_per_MWh\": round(cf_result['reserve_mcp'], 6),\n            \"binding_lines\": cf_result['binding_lines']\n        },\n        \"impact_analysis\": {\n            \"cost_reduction_dollars_per_hour\": round(cost_reduction, 2),\n            \"buses_with_largest_lmp_drop\": [\n                {\n                    \"bus\": item['bus'],\n                    \"base_lmp\": round(item['base_lmp'], 6),\n                    \"cf_lmp\": round(item['cf_lmp'], 6),\n                    \"delta\": round(item['delta'], 6)\n                }\n                for item in top_3_drops\n            ],\n            \"congestion_relieved\": congestion_relieved\n        }\n    }\n\n    # Extract LMPs from power balance constraint duals\n    # For equality constraint (gen - load == B*theta*baseMVA),\n    # the dual represents $/MWh cost to serve additional load\n    # Sign convention: negate because constraint is (injection - load = flow)\n    # The dual of \"supply - demand = net_injection\" gives price as negative of dual\n    lmps = []\n    for i, pbc in enumerate(power_balance_constraints):\n        dual = pbc.dual_value\n        if dual is not None:\n            # LMP = -dual because of constraint formulation\n            # When we have: Pg - Pd = B*theta*baseMVA\n            # Additional demand at bus i means we need more generation, which costs money\n            lmp = -float(dual)\n        else:\n            lmp = 0.0\n        lmps.append(lmp)\n    lmps = np.array(lmps)","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nDC-OPF with Reserve Co-optimization for Market Analysis\nCompares base case with counterfactual scenario (20% increased line capacity on bus 64-1501)\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nimport cvxpy as cp\n\ndef load_network(filepath):\n    \"\"\"Load MATPOWER-format network from JSON\"\"\"\n    with open(filepath, 'r') as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n    return data\n\ndef run_dc_opf_with_reserves(data, line_capacity_override=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run DC-OPF with reserve co-optimization.\n\n    Args:\n        data: Network data dictionary\n        line_capacity_override: Dict of {(from_bus, to_bus): new_capacity} to override\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with solution results including LMPs and reserve MCP\n    \"\"\"\n    baseMVA = data['baseMVA']\n    buses = np.array(data['bus'])\n    gens = np.array(data['gen'])\n    branches = np.array(data['branch'])\n    gencosts = np.array(data['gencost'])\n    reserve_capacities = np.array(data['reserve_capacity'])\n    reserve_requirement = data['reserve_requirement']\n\n    n_bus = len(buses)\n    n_gen = len(gens)\n    n_branch = len(branches)\n\n    # Create bus number to index mapping\n    bus_num_to_idx = {int(buses[i, 0]): i for i in range(n_bus)}\n\n    # Find slack bus (type = 3)\n    slack_idx = None\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        if buses[i, 1] == 3:\n            slack_idx = i\n            break\n    if slack_idx is None:\n        slack_idx = 0  # Default to first bus\n\n    # Get generator bus indices\n    gen_bus_indices = [bus_num_to_idx[int(gens[g, 0])] for g in range(n_gen)]\n\n    # Get generator parameters (only in-service generators)\n    gen_status = gens[:, 7]\n    gen_pmax = gens[:, 8]  # MW\n    gen_pmin = gens[:, 9]  # MW\n\n    # Get load at each bus (column 2 is PD in MW)\n    bus_load = buses[:, 2].copy()  # MW\n\n    # Build susceptance matrix B and branch data\n    B = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus))\n    branch_susceptances = []\n    branch_from_idx = []\n    branch_to_idx = []\n    branch_limits = []\n    branch_status = []\n    branch_from_bus = []\n    branch_to_bus = []\n\n    for i, br in enumerate(branches):\n        f_bus = int(br[0])\n        t_bus = int(br[1])\n        x = br[3]  # Reactance\n        rate_a = br[5]  # MW limit\n        status = br[10]\n\n        f = bus_num_to_idx[f_bus]\n        t = bus_num_to_idx[t_bus]\n\n        branch_from_idx.append(f)\n        branch_to_idx.append(t)\n        branch_status.append(status)\n        branch_from_bus.append(f_bus)\n        branch_to_bus.append(t_bus)\n\n        # Check for line capacity override\n        if line_capacity_override is not None:\n            if (f_bus, t_bus) in line_capacity_override:\n                rate_a = line_capacity_override[(f_bus, t_bus)]\n            elif (t_bus, f_bus) in line_capacity_override:\n                rate_a = line_capacity_override[(t_bus, f_bus)]\n\n        branch_limits.append(rate_a)\n\n        if x != 0 and status == 1:\n            b = 1.0 / x\n            B[f, f] += b\n            B[t, t] += b\n            B[f, t] -= b\n            B[t, f] -= b\n            branch_susceptances.append(b)\n        else:\n            branch_susceptances.append(0.0)\n\n    branch_susceptances = np.array(branch_susceptances)\n    branch_from_idx = np.array(branch_from_idx)\n    branch_to_idx = np.array(branch_to_idx)\n    branch_limits = np.array(branch_limits)\n    branch_status = np.array(branch_status)\n\n    # Decision variables\n    Pg = cp.Variable(n_gen, name=\"Pg\")  # Generator real power output (MW)\n    Rg = cp.Variable(n_gen, name=\"Rg\")  # Generator reserve provision (MW)\n    theta = cp.Variable(n_bus, name=\"theta\")  # Bus voltage angles (radians)\n\n    # Cost function: polynomial cost (MODEL=2, NCOST=3 means c2*Pg^2 + c1*Pg + c0)\n    # gencost format: [MODEL, STARTUP, SHUTDOWN, NCOST, c2, c1, c0]\n    total_cost = 0\n    for g in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[g] == 1:\n            c2 = gencosts[g, 4]\n            c1 = gencosts[g, 5]\n            c0 = gencosts[g, 6]\n            # Quadratic cost: c2*Pg^2 + c1*Pg + c0\n            total_cost += c2 * cp.square(Pg[g]) + c1 * Pg[g] + c0\n\n    # Constraints - store references for dual extraction\n    constraints = []\n\n    # 1. Slack bus angle reference\n    slack_constraint = theta[slack_idx] == 0\n    constraints.append(slack_constraint)\n\n    # 2. Generator limits\n    gen_constraints = []\n    for g in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[g] == 1:\n            constraints.append(Pg[g] >= gen_pmin[g])\n            constraints.append(Pg[g] + Rg[g] <= gen_pmax[g])\n            constraints.append(Rg[g] >= 0)\n            constraints.append(Rg[g] <= reserve_capacities[g])\n        else:\n            constraints.append(Pg[g] == 0)\n            constraints.append(Rg[g] == 0)\n\n    # 3. Power balance at each bus - store these separately for LMP extraction\n    # At each bus: Pg - Pd = B @ theta * baseMVA\n    gen_at_bus = np.zeros((n_bus, n_gen))\n    for g in range(n_gen):\n        gen_at_bus[gen_bus_indices[g], g] = 1.0\n\n    power_balance_constraints = []\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        gen_injection = gen_at_bus[i, :] @ Pg\n        pbc = gen_injection - bus_load[i] == B[i, :] @ theta * baseMVA\n        power_balance_constraints.append(pbc)\n        constraints.append(pbc)\n\n    # 4. Line flow limits\n    line_flow_constraints = []\n    active_branch_indices = []\n    for i in range(n_branch):\n        if branch_status[i] == 1 and branch_limits[i] > 0:\n            f = branch_from_idx[i]\n            t = branch_to_idx[i]\n            b = branch_susceptances[i]\n            flow = b * (theta[f] - theta[t]) * baseMVA\n            c1 = flow <= branch_limits[i]\n            c2 = flow >= -branch_limits[i]\n            line_flow_constraints.append((c1, c2, i))\n            active_branch_indices.append(i)\n            constraints.append(c1)\n            constraints.append(c2)\n\n    # 5. Reserve requirement\n    reserve_constraint = cp.sum(Rg) >= reserve_requirement\n    constraints.append(reserve_constraint)\n\n    # Solve the optimization problem with SCS (better for large problems)\n    problem = cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(total_cost), constraints)\n\n    # Try multiple solvers\n    solved = False\n    for solver in [cp.SCS, cp.OSQP, cp.CLARABEL]:\n        try:\n            if solver == cp.SCS:\n                problem.solve(solver=solver, verbose=False, max_iters=10000, eps=1e-5)\n            elif solver == cp.OSQP:\n                problem.solve(solver=solver, verbose=False, eps_abs=1e-5, eps_rel=1e-5, max_iter=10000)\n            else:\n                problem.solve(solver=solver, verbose=False)\n\n            if problem.status in ['optimal', 'optimal_inaccurate']:\n                solved = True\n                break\n        except Exception as e:\n            continue\n\n    if not solved:\n        raise RuntimeError(f\"Optimization failed with status: {problem.status}\")\n\n    # Extract results\n    Pg_opt = Pg.value\n    Rg_opt = Rg.value\n    theta_opt = theta.value\n\n    # Extract LMPs from power balance constraint duals\n    lmps = []\n    for i, pbc in enumerate(power_balance_constraints):\n        dual = pbc.dual_value\n        if dual is not None:\n            # LMP = marginal cost of supplying one more MW at this bus\n            # Sign convention: positive dual means higher cost to serve load\n            lmp = float(dual)\n        else:\n            lmp = 0.0\n        lmps.append(lmp)\n    lmps = np.array(lmps)\n\n    # Extract reserve MCP from reserve requirement constraint dual\n    reserve_mcp = reserve_constraint.dual_value\n    if reserve_mcp is None:\n        reserve_mcp = 0.0\n    else:\n        reserve_mcp = float(reserve_mcp)\n\n    # Calculate line flows and identify binding lines\n    line_flows = []\n    binding_lines = []\n    for i in range(n_branch):\n        if branch_status[i] == 1:\n            f = branch_from_idx[i]\n            t = branch_to_idx[i]\n            b = branch_susceptances[i]\n            flow = b * (theta_opt[f] - theta_opt[t]) * baseMVA\n            line_flows.append(flow)\n\n            loading = abs(flow) / branch_limits[i] * 100 if branch_limits[i] > 0 else 0\n            if loading >= 99.0:\n                f_bus = branch_from_bus[i]\n                t_bus = branch_to_bus[i]\n                binding_lines.append({\n                    'from': f_bus,\n                    'to': t_bus,\n                    'flow_MW': round(abs(flow), 2),\n                    'limit_MW': round(branch_limits[i], 2),\n                    'loading_pct': round(loading, 2)\n                })\n        else:\n            line_flows.append(0.0)\n\n    # Build LMP by bus result\n    lmp_by_bus = []\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        bus_num = int(buses[i, 0])\n        lmp_by_bus.append({\n            'bus': bus_num,\n            'lmp_dollars_per_MWh': round(lmps[i], 6)\n        })\n\n    return {\n        'total_cost': problem.value,\n        'lmp_by_bus': lmp_by_bus,\n        'lmps': lmps,\n        'reserve_mcp': reserve_mcp,\n        'binding_lines': binding_lines,\n        'Pg': Pg_opt,\n        'Rg': Rg_opt,\n        'theta': theta_opt,\n        'buses': buses,\n        'status': problem.status\n    }\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading network data...\")\n    data = load_network('/root/network.json')\n\n    print(f\"Network: {data['name']}\")\n    print(f\"Buses: {len(data['bus'])}, Generators: {len(data['gen'])}, Branches: {len(data['branch'])}\")\n    print(f\"Reserve requirement: {data['reserve_requirement']} MW\")\n\n    # Find the target line (bus 64 to bus 1501)\n    target_line_idx = None\n    original_capacity = None\n    for i, br in enumerate(data['branch']):\n        if (int(br[0]) == 64 and int(br[1]) == 1501) or (int(br[0]) == 1501 and int(br[1]) == 64):\n            target_line_idx = i\n            original_capacity = br[5]\n            print(f\"\\nTarget line: Bus {int(br[0])} -> Bus {int(br[1])}\")\n            print(f\"Original capacity: {original_capacity} MW\")\n            break\n\n    # Run base case\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n    print(\"Running BASE CASE scenario...\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n    base_result = run_dc_opf_with_reserves(data)\n    print(f\"Base case status: {base_result['status']}\")\n    print(f\"Base case total cost: ${base_result['total_cost']:,.2f}/hour\")\n    print(f\"Base case reserve MCP: ${base_result['reserve_mcp']:.4f}/MWh\")\n    print(f\"Number of binding lines: {len(base_result['binding_lines'])}\")\n\n    # Run counterfactual with 20% increased capacity\n    new_capacity = original_capacity * 1.20\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n    print(f\"Running COUNTERFACTUAL scenario (capacity: {original_capacity} -> {new_capacity} MW)...\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n    cf_result = run_dc_opf_with_reserves(data, line_capacity_override={(64, 1501): new_capacity})\n    print(f\"Counterfactual status: {cf_result['status']}\")\n    print(f\"Counterfactual total cost: ${cf_result['total_cost']:,.2f}/hour\")\n    print(f\"Counterfactual reserve MCP: ${cf_result['reserve_mcp']:.4f}/MWh\")\n    print(f\"Number of binding lines: {len(cf_result['binding_lines'])}\")\n\n    # Impact analysis\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n    print(\"IMPACT ANALYSIS\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n\n    cost_reduction = base_result['total_cost'] - cf_result['total_cost']\n    print(f\"Cost reduction: ${cost_reduction:,.2f}/hour\")\n\n    # Find buses with largest LMP drop\n    base_lmps = {item['bus']: item['lmp_dollars_per_MWh'] for item in base_result['lmp_by_bus']}\n    cf_lmps = {item['bus']: item['lmp_dollars_per_MWh'] for item in cf_result['lmp_by_bus']}\n\n    lmp_changes = []\n    for bus in base_lmps:\n        delta = cf_lmps[bus] - base_lmps[bus]\n        lmp_changes.append({\n            'bus': bus,\n            'base_lmp': base_lmps[bus],\n            'cf_lmp': cf_lmps[bus],\n            'delta': delta\n        })\n\n    #
